# AI Post-Training Economy: Canonical Research Synthesis

> Generated deterministically from `research/corpus/*.json`. Do not hand-edit this file.

## Method and scope

- Evidence cutoff: 2026-07-11.
- Corpus: 235 sources, 235 atomic observations, 561 claims, 10 named-company dossiers, and 63 bounded adjacent records.
- Strategy method: 70 executed positive/countersearch receipts across 5 vertical candidates and 7 fixed dimensions. Every lane was searched on 2026-07-12 against the 2026-07-11 cutoff; zero lanes remain `open_unverified`. The four business-entry models remain qualitative hypotheses.
- Provenance: root seed SHA-256 `b3a7652702e7d08759084eded15f9fdee23c638c122c6862aab791a99a41f001`; 77 seed sources and 218 cited seed anchors are mapped or rejected in the migration ledger.
- The 63-record adjacent census is quota-bounded and cutoff-locked. A separate post-cutoff discovery register reconciles 50 inputs into 47 additions, 2 core merges, and 1 unresolved lead without altering core scores or quotas.
- An official offer page proves only that an offer was advertised, not adoption, efficacy, revenue, or active legal status.

## Highest-signal market findings

- The early commercial unit included API-mediated human tasks and managed labeling work. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [YC: Scale is an API for human labor &#40;2016&#41;](https://www.ycombinator.com/blog/scale/)
- Interactive web and computer benchmarks shifted evaluation toward stateful tasks and held-out scoring. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [WebArena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.13854); [OSWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07972); [tau-bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12045)
- An RL environment supplies resettable state, actions, observations, tasks, logging, and verification; a benchmark is a held-out suite and scoring protocol. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [OpenEnv standard](https://huggingface.co/openenv)
- A narrow paid pilot tests acceptance criteria before a larger managed program. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)
- Gross marketplace volume, net revenue, software ARR, contract ceilings, and registered network size have different denominators and cannot be ranked as one metric. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [Mercor gross run rate and contractor activity](https://www.mercor.com/blog/when-you-go-from-2-million-a-month-to-2-million-a-day/); [Mercor gross/net economics reporting](https://www.theinformation.com/briefings/exclusive-mercor-hit-1-billion-annualized-revenue-breach); [Appen FY2025 results](https://announcements.asx.com.au/asxpdf/20260225/pdf/06wq7806yk1ll4.pdf); [TaskUs FY2025 results](https://ir.taskus.com/static-files/b6125cd3-8571-40f2-a8f0-0e7033fff501)
- A narrow, decision-relevant assurance pack preserves a possible wedge after horizontal platform countersearch. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)

## Value-chain edges

- **Expert data operations → RLHF:** Expert-created demonstrations and preferences can provide post-training signal. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Appen FY2025 results](https://announcements.asx.com.au/asxpdf/20260225/pdf/06wq7806yk1ll4.pdf); [Anthropic helpful and harmless RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.05862)
- **RLHF → Direct Preference Optimization:** Preference data can be consumed by optimization methods such as DPO without making the two terms equivalent. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Anthropic helpful and harmless RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.05862); [Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18290)
- **RL environments → RLVR:** Stateful environments can expose checkable outcomes used as verifiable rewards. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [OpenEnv standard](https://huggingface.co/openenv); [Prime Intellect environment contracts](https://docs.primeintellect.ai/verifiers/environments)
- **RLVR → Evaluations and assurance:** Verifiers and hidden tasks can support evaluation, but construct validity still requires independent review. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Prime Intellect environment contracts](https://docs.primeintellect.ai/verifiers/environments); [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/)
- **Computer use → Evaluations and assurance:** Computer-use systems require closed-loop evaluation over actions and state, not only final text. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [OSWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07972); [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/)
- **Evaluations and assurance → AX consulting:** Evaluation evidence can inform transformation decisions without itself completing organizational change. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [Agent Experience definition](https://agentexperience.ax/)

## Market history

- **2016-01-01:** The early commercial unit included API-mediated human tasks and managed labeling work. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [YC: Scale is an API for human labor &#40;2016&#41;](https://www.ycombinator.com/blog/scale/)
- **2022-04-12:** RLHF research formalized human preference signal as a model post-training input. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Anthropic helpful and harmless RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.05862)
- **2023-05-29:** DPO introduced an offline preference-optimization objective distinct from the provenance of feedback. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18290)
- **2023-07-01:** Interactive web and computer benchmarks shifted evaluation toward stateful tasks and held-out scoring. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [WebArena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.13854); [OSWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07972); [tau-bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12045)
- **2026-01-01:** Industry interviews reported active demand for bespoke RL environments while leaving category size and margins unresolved. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
- **2026-05-01:** Frontier-lab and public-sector materials documented demand for decision-relevant third-party evaluation and assurance. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · historical · routine_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [Anthropic third-party evaluation initiative](https://www.anthropic.com/news/a-new-initiative-for-developing-third-party-model-evaluations); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap)

## GTM motion

1. **Technical relationship:** Technical or founder relationships often precede formal procurement for bespoke data and environment work. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)
2. **Diagnostic or sample:** A bounded diagnostic, NDA, or sample turns a capability gap into an inspectable task specification. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)
3. **Paid pilot:** A narrow paid pilot tests acceptance criteria before a larger managed program. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)
4. **Acceptance proof:** Held-out results and buyer acceptance create the evidence required for expansion. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)
5. **Embedded expansion:** Forward-deployed delivery and adjacent work expand only after the initial proof clears buyer review. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · routine_ — [Micro1 business-data expansion case](https://www.micro1.ai/case-study/business-data); [Turing RL environments](https://www.turing.com/advance/rl-environments)

## Seven operating models

| Business model | Formula | Comparable unit | Evidence-backed interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| managed data bpo | `buyer price - creator payout - review - rework - runtime - variable support` | accepted artifact | Managed data / BPO contribution is modeled as buyer price - creator payout - review - rework - runtime - variable support; it should be compared on accepted artifact, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Appen FY2025 results](https://announcements.asx.com.au/asxpdf/20260225/pdf/06wq7806yk1ll4.pdf); [TaskUs FY2025 results](https://ir.taskus.com/static-files/b6125cd3-8571-40f2-a8f0-0e7033fff501) |
| expert marketplace | `buyer spend - expert payout - payments - trust and safety` | gross marketplace spend | Expert marketplace contribution is modeled as buyer spend - expert payout - payments - trust and safety; it should be compared on gross marketplace spend, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Prolific pricing](https://www.prolific.com/pricing) |
| employee bpo | `revenue - payroll - facilities - management - idle capacity` | managed employee hour | Employee BPO contribution is modeled as revenue - payroll - facilities - management - idle capacity; it should be compared on managed employee hour, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Appen FY2025 results](https://announcements.asx.com.au/asxpdf/20260225/pdf/06wq7806yk1ll4.pdf); [TaskUs FY2025 results](https://ir.taskus.com/static-files/b6125cd3-8571-40f2-a8f0-0e7033fff501) |
| expert environment services | `milestone revenue - expert time - engineering - review - compute` | accepted environment milestone | Expert + environment services contribution is modeled as milestone revenue - expert time - engineering - review - compute; it should be compared on accepted environment milestone, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Halluminate product/expert operating model](https://www.halluminate.ai/experts); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs) |
| eval observability saas | `subscription and usage revenue - judge inference - storage - support` | trace, run, or seat | Eval / observability SaaS contribution is modeled as subscription and usage revenue - judge inference - storage - support; it should be compared on trace, run, or seat, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Braintrust pricing](https://www.braintrust.dev/pricing); [LangSmith pricing](https://www.langchain.com/pricing) |
| runtime infrastructure | `usage revenue - compute - browser - proxy - network - orchestration` | runtime minute or session | Runtime infrastructure contribution is modeled as usage revenue - compute - browser - proxy - network - orchestration; it should be compared on runtime minute or session, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Browserbase pricing](https://www.browserbase.com/pricing); [AgentCore pricing](https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/agentcore/pricing/) |
| proprietary data acquisition | `license revenue - rights acquisition - cleaning - security - provenance` | licensed asset or dataset | Proprietary data acquisition contribution is modeled as license revenue - rights acquisition - cleaning - security - provenance; it should be compared on licensed asset or dataset, not conflated with gross volume or unrelated SaaS ARR. _inference · not publicly verified · medium · current · material_ — [Turing Project Lazarus](https://research.turing.com/project-lazarus) |

## Roadmap and pricing experiments

- **0-30 days:** Choose one externally decision-relevant workflow and secure a buyer-controlled discovery path. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs) Gate: Advance only with a named decision owner and lawful sample access. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
- **31-60 days:** Build a private baseline, task specification, verifier prototype, and adversarial validity test. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs) Gate: Advance only if false-pass risks and expert disagreement are measured. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
- **61-90 days:** Run a paid blinded pilot and deliver a signed technical evidence report with explicit limitations. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs) Gate: Advance only if the evidence changes or materially informs the buyer&#39;s decision. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
- **3-12 months:** Version the assurance pack, refresh hidden tasks, and integrate with buyer-selected runtime and evaluation systems. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs) Gate: Scale only after repeat or expansion evidence from at least two buyers. _recommendation · not publicly verified · medium · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
- **Paid discovery sprint experiment:** amount **unscored**; unit: bounded paid discovery sprint. Hypothesis: A paid discovery sprint is a pricing experiment for qualifying access, decision ownership, and verifier feasibility; no public evidence sets a universal price. _recommendation · not publicly verified · unscored · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK procurement award evidence](https://www.find-tender.service.gov.uk/Notice/037042-2025/PDF) Success: The experiment succeeds only when the buyer funds access and accepts a measurable decision artifact. _recommendation · not publicly verified · unscored · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK procurement award evidence](https://www.find-tender.service.gov.uk/Notice/037042-2025/PDF) Kill: Stop the pricing experiment when the buyer will not fund discovery or cannot grant lawful access to a decision-relevant workflow. _recommendation · not publicly verified · unscored · current · high risk_ — [OpenAI third-party evaluation playbook](https://openai.com/index/trustworthy-third-party-evaluations-foundations/); [UK trusted third-party AI assurance roadmap](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap/trusted-third-party-ai-assurance-roadmap); [Epoch AI RL environments industry interviews](https://epoch.ai/gradient-updates/state-of-rl-envs)
